11 INCOME TAX CHARGES (Continued)                                               11 稅項支出（續）
     On 22 December 2025, the Ministry of Finance (MOF) and the                   2025年12月22日，中 國 財 政 部 與 稅 務 總 局 發
     State Taxation Administration (STA) issued the “Notice on Matters            佈《關 於 保 險 合 同 準 則 轉 換 有 關 企 業 所 得 稅
     Concerning Enterprise Income Tax Treatment in Relation to the                處理事項的公告》     （財政部稅務總局公告2025
     Transition to the Insurance Contracts Standard” (MOF and STA                 年第15號） ，明確現就企業在執行中國財政部
     Announcement No.15 [2025]), clarified the enterprise income tax              《企 業 會 計 準 則 第25號－保 險 合 同》（ 財 會
     treatment in the course of enterprises’ implementation of Accounting         〔2020〕20號）過程中有關企業所得稅處理事
     Standard for the Business Enterprises No.25 – Insurance Contracts            項。上述公告適用於本集團中國內地保險企業。
     (Cai Kuai [2020] No.20) issued by the MOF. This notice is applicable
     to the insurance enterprises incorporated in Mainland China of the
     Group.

     The Group is subject to the global minimum top-up tax under the              本集團適用經濟合作及發展組織發佈關於支

27 FAIR VALUES OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS                                          27 金融工具的公允價值
     (a) Fair value of Group’s financial assets and financial                     (a) 本集團經常性以公允價值計量的金融資
         liabilities that are measured at fair value on a recurring                   產及金融負債的公允價值
         basis

          The fair value of financial assets and liabilities are determined as      金融資產及金融負債的公允價值按以下
          follows:                                                                  方式釐定：

          –    The fair value of financial assets and financial liabilities         –   歸類為第一級為於活躍流動市場交易
               classified as Level 1 with standard terms and conditions                 並受標準條款及條件規管之金融資產
               and traded on active liquid markets are determined with                  及金融負債，其公允價值分別參照近
               reference to recent transaction price or quoted prices                   期交易價格或標價（不做調整）釐定；
               (unadjusted) respectively;

          –    The fair value of derivative instruments are estimated using         –   衍生工具的公允價值乃根據折算現金

               casualty insurance business in the PRC, the investment                 理的《投資程序手冊》包括中國國家

               and rectification action will be taken immediately.

               securities since the investment portfolio is well diversified.         易對手信用風險。

               insurance creditor balances with the same reinsurer.                   排持有的。

               Group evaluates the credit risk at initial recognition and             估初始確認時的信用風險，以及每個
               also whether there is any significant increase in credit risk          報告期的信用風險是否有任何大幅增
               for each reporting period.                                             加。

               assets with similar credit risk characteristics to determine           信用風險特徵的金融資產，通過比較
               ECL staging by comparing the credit risk of the financial              金融資產在報告日的信用風險和初始
               asset at reporting date with the credit risk at initial                確認時的信用風險來確定預期信用減
               recognition. Various reasonable supporting information are             值損失的階段。在確定金融資產的預
               used to judge if there is significant increase in credit risk,         期信用減值損失階段時，各種合理的
               including forward-looking information, when determining                支持資訊會被用來判斷信用風險是否
               the ECL staging for financial assets.                                  有顯著增加，包括前瞻性的資訊。

               (Continued)

               The Group set quantitative and qualitative criteria to identify         本集團制定了定量和定性的標準來識
               whether the financial asset has significant increase in credit          別金融資產自初始確認以來是否有顯
               risk since initial recognition. Major factors being considered          著的信用風險增加。考慮的主要因素
               is the probability of default upon initial recognition of               是金融資產初始確認時的違約概率，
               financial asset and whether there has been ongoing                      以及在每個報告期間違約概率是否持
               increase in probability of default throughout each reporting            續增加。本集團根據現有的合理和支
               period. The Group assess significant increase in credit risk            持性的前瞻性資訊，評估每個報告日
               as at each reporting date based on available reasonable                 的信用風險的顯著增加，如但不限於：
               and supportive forward-looking information such as but not
               limited to:

               –    External credit rating (as far as available);                      –   外部信用評級（如可用）
                                                                                                     ；

               –    Actual or expected significant adverse changes in                  –   業務、財務或經濟狀況的實際或
                    business, financial or economic conditions that are                    預期的重大不利變化，預計會導
                    expected to cause a significant change to the issuer’s                 致發行人履行義務的能力發生重
                    ability to meet its obligations;                                       大變化；

               –    Actual or expected significant changes in the operating            –   發行人經營業績的實際或預期的
                    results of the issuer;                                                 重大變化；

               –    Significant increase in credit risk on other financial             –   同一發行人發行的其他金融工具
                    instruments issued by the same issuer; and                             的信用風險顯著增加；以及

               –    Significant changes in the value of the collateral                 –   支持金融資產的抵押品的價值或
                    supporting the financial asset or the quality or third                 第三方擔保或增信措施的品質發
                    party guarantees or credit enhancements.                               生重大變化。

               In the judgement of whether the financial instruments have              在判斷金融工具在初始確認後信用
               significant increase in credit risks after initial recognition,         風險是否顯著增加時，本集團根據香
               the Group considers the 30 days (inclusive) past due as                 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第9號，將 逾 期30天
               one of criteria of significant increase in credit risks, in            （ 含）視 為 信 用 風 險 顯 著 增 加 的 標 準
               accordance with HKFRS 9.                                                之一。

               Judgement of credit-impaired assets                                 對信用受損資產的判斷

               At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                  在每個報告日，集團評估以攤餘成本
               financial assets measured at amortised cost, debt                   計量的金融資產、以公允價值計量且
               investments at FVOCI and lease receivables are credit-              其變動計入其他綜合收益的債務投資
               impaired. A financial asset is credit impaired when one             和應收金融租賃款是否信用受損。當
               or more events that have a detrimental impact on the                一個或多個以下事件對金融資產的預
               estimated future cash flows of the financial asset have             期未來現金流造成減少時，該金融資
               occurred.                                                           產的信用受損。

               Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired includes         一個金融資產的信用受損的證據包括
               the following observable data:                                      以下可觀測數據：

               –    Internal credit rating is default grade; or                    –   內部信用評級為損失級；或

               –    The lender gives the borrower concessions for                  –   借款人希望債權人因財政困難做
                    economic or contractual reasons due to the debtor                  出經濟上或合約上的讓步，但債
                    financial difficulties, where such concessions are                 權人通常難以接受此讓步；或
                    normally reluctant to be made by the borrower; or

               –    Significant financial difficulty of the issuer or              –   發債人有顯著的財政困難；或
                    counterparty; or

               –    It becoming probable that the borrower will enter              –   借款人有可能破產或重組；或
                    bankruptcy or financial re-organisation; or

               –    Disappearance of an active market for that financial           –   因財政困難，該金融資產不再在
                    asset because of financial difficulties.                           活躍市場上交易。

               A financial asset that has been renegotiated due to                 通常，由於借款人狀況惡化而重新商
               a deterioration in the borrower’s condition is usually              定的金融資產通常被視為信用受損，
               considered to be credit-impaired unless there is evidence           除非有證據表明無法獲得合同現金流
               that the risk of not receiving contractual cash flows has           的風險已大幅降低，並且沒有其他減
               reduced significantly and there are no other indicators of          值跡象。
               impairment.

               Judgement of credit-impaired assets (Continued)                         對信用受損資產的判斷（ 續）

               In assessing whether an investment in sovereign debt is                 在評估一項主權債務投資是否信用受
               credit-impaired, the Group considers the following factors:             損時，本集團考慮以下因素：

               –    the market’s assessment of creditworthiness as                     –   債券收益率所反映的市場對信用
                    reflected in bond yields;                                              度的評估；

               –    the rating agencies’ assessments of creditworthiness;              –   評級機構對信用度的評估；

               –    the country’s ability to access the capital markets for            –   該國家進入資本市場發行新債的
                    new debt issuance;                                                     能力；

               –    the probability of debt being restructured, resulting              –   債務重組的概率，通過自願或強
                    in holders suffering losses through voluntary or                       制債務豁免導致持有人遭受損
                    mandatory debt forgiveness; and                                        失；以及

               –    the international support mechanisms in place to                   –   作 為「最 後 貸 款 人」向 該 國 提 供
                    provide the necessary support as “Lender of Last                       必要支持的現有國際支持機制，
                    Resort” to that country, as well as the intention,                     以及反映在公開聲明中的政府和
                    reflected in public statements, of governments and                     機構使用這些機制的意圖，包括
                    agencies to use those mechanisms, including an                         評估這些機制的深度，以及無論
                    assessment of the depth of those mechanisms and,                       政治意圖如何，是否有能力滿足
                    irrespective of the political intent, whether there is the             所需標準。
                    capacity to fulfil the required criteria.

               The financial asset is considered credit-impaired when the              當 交 易 方 未 能 在 合 同 到 期 後90天 內
               counterparty fails to make contractual payments within 90               支付合同款項時，該金融資產被視為
               days of when they fall due.                                             信用受損。

               Inputs, assumptions and techniques used for estimating                 用於估計減值的輸入值、假設和技
               impairment                                                             術

               The parameters and assumptions involved in ECL model                   預期信用減值損失模型涉及的參數和
               are described below:                                                   假設描述如下：

               For financial assets with or without significant increase in           對於信用風險有或沒有顯著增加的
               credit risk, lifetime or 12 months expected credit losses              金融資產，分別提供整個期限或12個
               are provided respectively. ECL is the result of discounting            月的預期信用減值損失。預期信用減
               the product of Exposure at Default (“EAD”), Probability at             值損失是對違約風險敞口（「EAD 」）       、
               Default (“PD”) and Loss Given Default (“LGD”).                         違 約 概 率（「 P D 」）和 違 約 損 失 率
                                                                                     （「LGD 」）的乘積進行折現的結果。

               EAD: EAD is based on the amounts of the Group expects                  EAD：EAD是 基 於 在 違 約 時，本 集 團
               to be owed at the time of default, over the next 12 months             在 未 來12個 月 內 或 在 剩 餘 期 限 內 預
               or over the remaining lifetime.                                        期被虧欠的金額。

               PD: PD represents the likelihood of a borrower defaulting              PD：PD代 表 借 款 人 在 未 來12個月 內
               on its financial obligation, either over the next 12 months or         或在債務的剩餘期限內違約的可能
               over the remaining lifetime of the obligation, depending on            性，這取決於金融資產自初始確認以
               whether the financial asset has significant increase in credit         來信用風險是否大幅增加或被評估為
               risk since initial recognition or is assessed to be credit-            上述的信用受損。每個內部信用評級
               impaired as described above. PD for each internal credit               的PD由集團的信用評級中心確定，並
               rating is determined by the Group’s Credit Rating Center               每年審查。
               and is reviewed annually.

               LGD: LGD represents the Group’s expectation of the                     LGD：LGD代表本集團對違約風險的
               extent of loss on default exposure. LGD varies type of                 損失範圍的預期。LGD因金融資產的
               financial asset, type of counterparty, seniority of claim              類 型、交 易 對 手 的 類 型、賠 款 的 優 先
               and availability of collateral or other credit support. LGD            權和抵押品或其他信貸支持的可用性
               is expressed as a percentage loss per unit of exposure at              而 不 同。LGD以EAD的 每 單 位 風 險 的
               EAD. The Group determines LGD taking into consideration                損失百分比表示。本集團在確定LGD
               publications by Basel Committee on Banking Supervision                 時考慮了巴塞爾銀行監管委員會和穆
               and Moody’s, adjusted based on the financial condition of              迪的出版物，並根據借款人的財務狀
               the borrower and the Group’s experience studies.                       況和本集團的經驗研究進行調整。

               The Group makes adjustment to the probability of default               本集團考慮到歷史違約率，根據宏觀
               taking into consideration historical default rates and                 經濟前瞻性數據，對違約概率進行調
               adjusts for forward-looking macroeconomic data. There                  整。本財務期對於估計調整的技術和
               were no significant changes to estimation techniques or                假設沒有發生重大變化。
               assumptions made during the Period.

          (iii) Liquidity risk                                                                      (iii) 流 動 性 風 險

               notably from claims arising from its life insurance contracts,                           用，特 別 是 來 自 人 壽 保 險 合 同、財 產

               contracts. There is, therefore, a risk that cash will not be                             費用。因此存在現金不足以償還到期
               available to settle liabilities when due.                                                負債的風險。

               The Group manages this risk by formulating policies and                                  本集團透過制定流動資金管理的政策
               general strategies of liquidity management to ensure that                                及一般策略來管理該風險，以確保本
               the Group can meet its financial obligations in normal                                   集團在正常情況下能夠履行其財務義
               circumstances and that an adequate stock of high-quality                                 務，並保持足夠的高質量流動性資產
               liquid assets is maintained in order to contain the possibility                          儲備，以應對可能發生的流動資金危
               of a liquidity crisis.                                                                   機。

               A p a r t f ro m l i q u i d i t y m a n a g e m e n t a n d re g u l a t o r y          除了流動資金管理和監管合規外，本
               compliance, the Group always strives to maintain a                                       集團致力於留存適度的流動資金緩衝
               comfortable liquidity cushion as a safety net for coping with                            額，作為應對意料之外的大筆資金需
               unexpected large funding requirements and to maintain                                    求的安全措施，並保持一個應急計畫，

               company specific crisis.

business and reinsurance business. The Group also has other              有其他公司經營投資控股、資產管理、養老保
companies and operations in the areas of investment holding,             險及其他業務。太平人壽（本公司持有75.1%
asset management, pensions and other businesses. The life                股 權 之 附 屬 公 司）運 作 之 人 壽 保 險 分 部 就 其
insurance segment operated by TPL, a 75.1%-owned subsidiary,             毛承保保費額、總資產及盈利能力而言是本
is a significant part of the Group in terms of gross premiums            集團重要之部份。為向投資者提供額外資料
written, total assets and profitability. In order to provide investors   以評估太平人壽之盈利能力及估值，本集團
with additional information to evaluate the profitability and            於年度及中期業績公告內披露太平人壽之內
valuation of TPL, the Group discloses the Embedded Value                 含價值及新業務價值。內含價值包括經調整
and New Business Value of TPL in its Annual and Interim                  股東資產淨值及未來有效業務可為股東創造
Results Announcements. The Embedded Value consists of the                之預期現金流之現值，扣減為支持有效業務
shareholders’ adjusted net worth plus the present value of future        而按照監管要求持有償付資本之成本。新業
expected cash flows to shareholders from the in-force business,          務價值乃指以精算方法評估的在本財務期售
less the costs of holding regulatory solvency capital to support         出的人壽保險新業務所產生的經濟價值。
the in-force business. The New Business Value represents an
actuarially determined estimate of the economic value arising
from the new life insurance business issued during the Period.

develop well. To provide investors with further information on           關以上營運的資料，本集團披露集團的總內
these operations, the Group also discloses the Group Embedded            含價值。總內含價值乃定義為其他核心業務
Value. The Group Embedded Value is defined as the Adjusted               的經調整資產淨值，加上太平人壽的內含價
Net Worth of the Other Core Operations plus the Embedded                 值。其他核心業務的經調整資產淨值按香港

Operations is determined by Hong Kong Financial Reporting                整。請注意總內含價值的計算並不包括日後
Standards, with marked-to-market and goodwill adjustments.               任何新業務的估值。

core businesses in preparing the Group Embedded Value.


CAUTIONARY STATEMENT                                                   提示聲明

Report on Review of Interim Financial Information

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                                       審閱工作範圍

We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong                  我們的審閱工作是按照香港會計師公會頒佈
Standard on Review Engagements 2410, Review of interim                的 香 港 審 閱 準 則 第2410號「獨 立 核 數 師 對 企
financial information performed by the independent auditor of the     業中期財務信息執行審閱」的要求進行的。中
entity as issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public       期財務報告的審閱工作主要包括向財務會計
Accountants. A review of interim financial report consists of         負責人進行查詢，執行分析性覆核及其他審
making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial      閱程序。由於審閱的範圍遠小於按照香港審
and accounting matters, and applying analytical and other review      計準則進行審計的範圍，所以不能保證我們
procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit     會注意到在審計中可能會被發現的所有重大
conducted in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing          事項。因此，我們不發表審計意見。
and consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that
we would become aware of all significant matters that might be
identified in an audit. Accordingly we do not express an audit
opinion.


CONCLUSION                                                            結論

Based on our review, nothing has come to our attention that           根據我們的審閱，我們沒有注意到任何事項
causes us to believe that the interim financial report of the Group   使 我 們 相 信 於2026年6月30日 的 中 期 財 務 報
as at 30 June 2026 is not prepared, in all material respects, in      告在所有重大方面沒有按照香港會計準則第
accordance with Hong Kong Accounting Standard 34 Interim              34號「中期財務報告」編製。
financial reporting .
